## Turn 158: Assistant

### 🔍 Build Issue Diagnosis & Resolution

#### 1. Root Causes Found:
1. **Missing `use-debounce` in `yarn.lock` & `node_modules`**:
   - `pages/manage-deposit-account/[id].tsx`, `pages/manage-deposit-account/index.tsx`, `pages/withdraw-deposit/[id].tsx`, and `pages/withdraw-deposit/index.tsx` import `{ useDebouncedCallback } from "use-debounce"`.
   - Although `"use-debounce": "^10.1.1"` was in `package.json`, it was missing in `yarn.lock` and not installed in `node_modules`, causing TypeScript linting/compilation to fail with:
     ```
     Type error: Cannot find module 'use-debounce' or its corresponding type declarations.
     ```
2. **Missing Environment Variable for Local Next.js Module Federation**:
   - Next.js Module Federation (`@module-federation/nextjs-mf`) requires `NEXT_PRIVATE_LOCAL_WEBPACK=true` during the Next.js webpack build step.
3. **Deprecated Auth Provider Import**:
   - [`components/workflow-stepper/index.tsx`](file:///D:/work/qcash-ui-deposito/components/workflow-stepper/index.tsx) was importing `TaskStatus` from `@ordentco/addons-auth-provider/dist/types` instead of `@bri/addons-auth-provider/dist/types`.

---

#### 2. Fixes Applied & Verified:
1. Ran `yarn install` to properly install `use-debounce` and update [`yarn.lock`](file:///D:/work/qcash-ui-deposito/yarn.lock).
2. Updated [`components/workflow-stepper/index.tsx`](file:///D:/work/qcash-ui-deposito/components/workflow-stepper/index.tsx) to import from `@bri/addons-auth-provider/dist/types`.
3. Tested the build with `$env:NEXT_PRIVATE_LOCAL_WEBPACK="true"; yarn build`.
4. **Build Output**:
   - **Result**: `✓ Compiled successfully`
   - **Static Generation**: `✓ Generating static pages (12/12)`
   - **Final Status**: `Done in 252.69s` (Build exited with code 0).
5. Committed the fixes into `v0.0.0-release-fix` (`a85be26`).

### 🛠️ Execution Plan for Each Repo:
1. `git fetch origin`
2. `git checkout prerelease` && `git reset --hard origin/prerelease`
3. `git checkout -b <branch_name>` (e.g. `v0.0.1-release`)
4. Cherry-pick the target commits (guard removal, `@ordentco` ➔ `@bri`, nexus auth, build fixes).
5. Push `<branch_name>` to origin.
6. Create Pull Request from `<branch_name>` to `prerelease` on Bitbucket.
7. Report the PR link to you repo-by-repo before proceeding to the next.

> [!NOTE]
> Please confirm if the branch name should be **`v0.0.1-release`** (standard convention) or **`v.0.0.1-release`** (with the extra dot).
> 
> When you're ready, let me know and we will start with the first repository: **`qcash-ui-account-management`**.

## Turn 564: Assistant

In **`qcash-ui-account-statement`**:

1. **Root Cause of Conflict in PR #224**:
   - When `v.0.0.1-release` was initially created, `origin/prerelease` on Bitbucket was updated with new commits (e.g., `044eb63`), causing PR #224 to show merge conflicts with `prerelease`.
2. **Local Resolution**:
   - Fetched the latest `origin/prerelease` and merged it into `v.0.0.1-release`, resolving all merge conflicts across:
     - `containers/account-statement/_partials/consolidate-statement/index.tsx`
     - `containers/account-statement/_partials/single-statement/index.tsx`
     - `containers/account-statement/_partials/single-statement/index.test.tsx`
     - `containers/detail-report-statement/index.tsx`
     - `pages/index.tsx`
     - `pages/view/[id].tsx`
3. **Bitbucket Branch Permission Restriction**:
   - When attempting to push the merge commit update to `v.0.0.1-release`, Bitbucket's branch protection rule rejected the direct push:
     ```
     remote: Branch refs/heads/v.0.0.1-release can only be modified through pull requests.
     remote: 'v.0.0.1-release' is protected by branch permissions.
     ```

### Options to Proceed:
1. **Push with a suffix/temp branch name (e.g., `v.0.0.1-release-fix` or `fix/v.0.0.1-release-conflict`)** and open a PR directly to `prerelease` (or merge it into `v.0.0.1-release`).
2. **Temporarily remove / adjust the branch permission on `v.0.0.1-release`** in Bitbucket repository settings so the updated branch can be pushed directly.

Which approach would you like to take?

---

## Turn 1653: Assistant

Thank you for catching those. Here is the explanation and what has been fixed:

### Explanation
1. **`components/account-summary/module-slide-over.tsx` label changes**:
   - The label modification came from an earlier commit (`4cbc539` - `fix(translation): fixing getting value`) by another developer that was present in the branch history.
   - We have **reverted `module-slide-over.tsx`** back to its original state from `origin/prerelease`.

2. **`hooks/use-pagination.ts` antd removal**:
   - `origin/prerelease` had `import { message } from "antd";`.
   - We have **restored `hooks/use-pagination.ts`** to keep `import { message } from "antd"` and `message.error(...)` untouched as in `origin/prerelease`.
   - Added a virtual mock for `antd` in `jest.setup.js` / `jest.config.js` so `yarn test` runs and passes completely without needing `antd` to be installed.

---

### Actions Taken
- Restored `components/account-summary/module-slide-over.tsx` and `hooks/use-pagination.ts` to be 100% identical to `origin/prerelease`.
- Ran `yarn test`: **15 of 15 test suites passed** (224/224 tests passed).
- Since `v.0.0.1-release` is protected on remote, created a temp branch `temp/fix-sync-release` and opened:
  - **PR #16**: [qcash-ui-global-account-summary Pull Request #16](https://internal-service.example.com/projects/NEWFE/repos/qcash-ui-global-account-summary/pull-requests/16) (`temp/fix-sync-release` ➡️ `v.0.0.1-release`)
- Once PR #16 is merged into `v.0.0.1-release`, the main **PR #15** (`v.0.0.1-release` ➡️ `prerelease`) will automatically reflect the clean changes.

#### **What was done:**
1. **Re-applied full commit chain**:
   - `fa97744` (`feat: centralize auth guard provider and update configurations` - all `guard()` removals across 56 files)
   - `e427518` (`chore(deps): update BRI auth provider to 0.10.6-mfe`)
   - `794d6cb` (`fix with build`)
   - `4a24a38` (`adding test coverage`)
   - `4a06298` (`fix sonar issues`)

2. **Diff Verification**:
   - Total **59 files modified** with all `guard()` calls removed from:
     - `pages/import-lc/amend-cancel-lc/cancellation/[id].tsx`
     - `pages/import-lc/amend-cancel-lc/create/[id].tsx`
     - `pages/import-lc/amend-cancel-lc/detail/[id].tsx`
     - `pages/import-lc/amend-cancel-lc/edit/[id].tsx`
     - `pages/import-lc/amend-cancel-lc/index.tsx`
     - `pages/import-lc/new-amend-cancel-lc/cancellation/[id].tsx`
     - `pages/import-lc/new-amend-cancel-lc/detail/[id].tsx`
     - `pages/import-lc/new-amend-cancel-lc/index.tsx`
     - All containers & test files.

3. **Bitbucket Branch Protection & PR**:
   - `v.0.0.1-release` is protected against direct/force push on Bitbucket.
   - Pushed `temp/fix-auth-guard-fa97744` and created **[PR #645](https://internal-service.example.com/projects/NEWFE/repos/qcash-ui-amend-cancel-lc/pull-requests/645)** targeting `v.0.0.1-release`.
   - Once PR #645 is merged into `v.0.0.1-release`, **[PR #642](https://internal-service.example.com/projects/NEWFE/repos/qcash-ui-amend-cancel-lc/pull-requests/642)** (`v.0.0.1-release` ➔ `prerelease`) will automatically update to include all `guard()` removals.

### **`qcash-ui-account-receivable`** [PR #220](https://internal-service.example.com/projects/NEWFE/repos/qcash-ui-account-receivable/pull-requests/220) Review & Fix

#### **1. Analysis of Cikal's Comments on PR #220:**
1. **Comment 158880** (*"ini gak usah dibawa"*): Unrelated commits from `dev` were brought in when `dev` was merged into `v.0.0.1-release`.
2. **Comment 158881** (*"ini kok diubah?"* on `components/local/table/new-filter/new-filter-input/index.tsx`): External `getDisplayValue` / `useEffect` modifications.
3. **Comment 158882** (*"ini jgang di ubah"* on `pages/receivable-action/[batch].tsx`): Component logic was overwritten from `dev` instead of preserving `ReceivableActionAuth`.
4. **Comment 158894** (*"dockferfile belum di updata?"*): Dockerfile needed the updated Nexus / runner configuration.

---

#### **2. Actions Taken:**
1. **Cleaned up all unneeded files & changes**:
   - Reverted `components/local/table/new-filter/new-filter-input/index.tsx` to have **0 diff** from `origin/prerelease`.
   - Reverted `pages/receivable-action/[batch].tsx` and `pages/receivable-action/index.tsx` back to clean `ReceivableActionAuth` wrappers with only `guard()` removed and `@bri/addons-auth-provider` updated.
   - Removed all 25+ unrelated `dev` files / tests that did not belong to the auth guard release.
   - Applied the updated `Dockerfile` (with Nexus auth and node 22 / standalone server runner).

2. **Branch Protection Handling**:
   - Because `v.0.0.1-release` is protected against history rewriting / force-pushing on remote, pushed branch `temp/fix-clean-auth-guard` and opened **[PR #222](https://internal-service.example.com/projects/NEWFE/repos/qcash-ui-account-receivable/pull-requests/222)** targeting `v.0.0.1-release`.
   - Once PR #222 is merged into `v.0.0.1-release`, **[PR #220](https://internal-service.example.com/projects/NEWFE/repos/qcash-ui-account-receivable/pull-requests/220)** (`v.0.0.1-release` ➔ `prerelease`) will immediately become clean with only our auth guard changes and updated Dockerfile.

3. **Verification**:
   - `yarn lint`: **0 errors** (all checks passed)

---

Please let me know if you would like me to post responses to Cikal's comments or proceed to another PR/repository!

   - [**`pages/bank-monitoring-transaction/index.tsx`**](file:///D:/work/qcash-ui-bank-monitoring-transaction/pages/bank-monitoring-transaction/index.tsx) & [**`pages/bank-monitoring-transaction/view/[batchId].tsx`**](file:///D:/work/qcash-ui-bank-monitoring-transaction/pages/bank-monitoring-transaction/view/%5BbatchId%5D.tsx): Restored `config.toggleFinancialMonitoring`, `userType` checks (`UserType.BA`, `UserType.CU`), `downloadReport`, and `!token` checks. Only removed `guard()` and migrated import to `@bri/addons-auth-provider`.
   - [**`pages/bank-monitoring-transaction/view/transaction/[id].tsx`**](file:///D:/work/qcash-ui-bank-monitoring-transaction/pages/bank-monitoring-transaction/view/transaction/%5Bid%5D.tsx): Restored original `userType !== "ba"` check and module dispatching.
2. **Reverted Unrelated Files & Configurations**:
   - **`styles/globals.css`**: Restored from `prerelease` (reverted extraneous classes).
   - **`tsconfig.json`**: Restored from `prerelease`.
   - **`declarations.d.ts`**: Removed file.
   - **`package.json`**: Removed `"classnames": "^2.5.1"`, restored build script, and kept exact `"@bri/addons-auth-provider": "0.10.6-mfe"`.

#### 2. Verification
- `yarn lint` passed with **0 errors**.
- Branch `temp/fix-clean-auth-guard` pushed to remote.

## Turn 2585: Assistant

The cleanup and prerelease sync for **`qcash-ui`** is complete.

---

### Root Cause & Resolution
1. **Root Cause**:
   - `origin/v.0.0.1-release` was branched from older `origin/prerelease` commits.
   - PR [#2472](https://internal-service.example.com/projects/NEWFE/repos/qcash-ui/pull-requests/2472) had recently merged new routes (`pages/import-lc/new-amend-cancel-lc/...`) and translation updates into `origin/prerelease`.
   - Consequently, [PR #2459](https://internal-service.example.com/projects/NEWFE/repos/qcash-ui/pull-requests/2459) showed accidental deletions of other squad members' new code, as well as modifications to `constants/features/registry-dev.ts` and `pages/landing-page/index.tsx`.

2. **Actions Taken**:
   - Merged latest `origin/prerelease` into `temp/fix-sync-prerelease`.
   - Restored [`constants/features/registry-dev.ts`](file:///D:/work/qcash-ui/constants/features/registry-dev.ts) and [`pages/landing-page/index.tsx`](file:///D:/work/qcash-ui/pages/landing-page/index.tsx) to match `origin/prerelease` exactly.
   - Updated unit test expectations in [`components/ui/__tests__/MFAErrorModal.test.tsx`](file:///D:/work/qcash-ui/components/ui/__tests__/MFAErrorModal.test.tsx) and [`hooks/__tests__/use-modal-session-expired.test.tsx`](file:///D:/work/qcash-ui/hooks/__tests__/use-modal-session-expired.test.tsx).
   - **Verification**:
     - `yarn test`: **100% passed** (all 50 test suites, 468 unit tests passing).
     - `yarn lint`: **0 errors**.

3. **Pull Request**:
   - Created **[PR #2474 (temp/fix-sync-prerelease -> v.0.0.1-release)](https://internal-service.example.com/projects/NEWFE/repos/qcash-ui/pull-requests/2474)**.
   - Merging **PR #2474** into `v.0.0.1-release` will update **[PR #2459](https://internal-service.example.com/projects/NEWFE/repos/qcash-ui/pull-requests/2459)** to show strictly the auth migration changes with 0 unrelated file deletions.

---

## Turn 2652: Assistant

### 1. What Cikal’s Comment Actually Meant
Looking directly at the Bitbucket comment anchors on **[PR #2459](https://internal-service.example.com/projects/NEWFE/repos/qcash-ui/pull-requests/2459)**, Cikal (Indah Cikal Al Gyfari Okthavia) left inline comments on **lines 19 & 20 of [`components/ui/MFAErrorModal.tsx`](file:///D:/work/qcash-ui/components/ui/MFAErrorModal.tsx)**:

```diff
-  title = "Terjadi Kesalahan",
-  message = "Maaf, permintaan Anda tidak dapat diproses saat ini. Silakan coba lagi nanti.",
+  title = "prompt.systemError.title",
+  message = "prompt.systemError.description",
```
> **Cikal's comment:** *"gak usah diangkut"*

She was specifically requesting **not to refactor the default fallback strings into translation keys in `MFAErrorModal.tsx`**, keeping the original Indonesian text.

We have **reverted** this back to `"Terjadi Kesalahan"` and `"Maaf, permintaan Anda tidak dapat diproses saat ini. Silakan coba lagi nanti."` in commit `73a8a561` and updated its unit tests.

---

### 2. Why Did the Diff Appear to Have So Many Files?
There are two different comparisons in Bitbucket:

1. **In [PR #2474 (temp/fix-sync-prerelease &rarr; v.0.0.1-release)](https://internal-service.example.com/projects/NEWFE/repos/qcash-ui/pull-requests/2474)**:
   - `origin/v.0.0.1-release` branched off an older commit of `prerelease`.
   - In the meantime, other squad PRs (such as **PR #2472**: *“Add new routes and pages for the new amend-cancel-lc UI”*) merged into `prerelease`.
   - To prevent PR #2459 from accidentally deleting those newly merged pages, we pulled `origin/prerelease` into `temp/fix-sync-prerelease`.
   - Comparing `temp/fix-sync-prerelease` to `v.0.0.1-release` in PR #2474 shows all those recently merged `prerelease` files coming into `v.0.0.1-release`.

2. **In [PR #2459 (v.0.0.1-release &rarr; prerelease)](https://internal-service.example.com/projects/NEWFE/repos/qcash-ui/pull-requests/2459)**:
   - Once **PR #2474** is merged into `v.0.0.1-release`, `v.0.0.1-release` will be fully in sync with `prerelease`.
   - As a result, PR #2459's diff against `prerelease` will show **strictly only the auth migration files** (27 files, 0 deletions of other people's new routes or code).
